-e "$prefix-$suffix"     done   done done source /usr/local/Ascend/ascend-toolkit/set_env.sh > "$P/v037-correctness-env.log" 2>&1 export LD_LIBRARY_PATH="/usr/lib/aarch64-linux-gnu:$LD_LIBRARY_PATH" npu-smi info > "$P/v037-pre-correctness-npu-smi.txt" 2>&1 ps -eo pid,ppid,user,comm,args --sort=pid > "$P/v037-pre-correctness-processes.txt" printf 'V037_CORRECTNESS_START_UTC=%s\nHOST=%s\nDEVICE=4\nPARENT=R31B-V011\nCANDIDATE_SHA256=b165a8d60e03ad620dc8129b97b433a2a18970b03ca43b539a0b19c2cb5e9f3c\nDTYPE=FP32\nSHAPES=128x2040,128x2048,128x2056\nC15=EXCLUDED\n' "$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S.%NZ)" "$(hostname)" > "$P/v037-correctness.log" printf 'timestamp_utc\tshape\tside\tbinary\trc\n' > "$P/v037-correctness-index.tsv" for width in 2040 2048 2056; do   for side in parent candidate; do     if [[ "$side" == parent ]]; then binary="$PARENT"; else binary="$CANDIDATE"; fi     prefix="$P/$side-correctness-r128-w$width-fp32"     printf 'RUN_BEGIN %s %s %s\n' "$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S.%NZ)" "$side" "$width" | tee -a "$P/v037-correctness.log"     if "$binary" 4 128 "$width" 0 "$prefix" 0 1 1 0 1 > "$prefix-stdout.log" 2>&1; then rc=0; else rc=$?; fi     printf '%s\t128x%s\t%s\t%s\t%s\n' "$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S.%NZ)" "$width" "$side" "$binary" "$rc" >> "$P/v037-correctness-index.tsv"     cat "$prefix-stdout.log" >> "$P/v037-correctness.log"     cat "$prefix-stats.txt" >> "$P/v037-correctness.log"     printf 'RUN_END rc=%s prefix=%s\n' "$rc" "$prefix" | tee -a "$P/v037-correctness.log"   done done npu-smi info > "$P/v037-post-correctness-npu-smi.txt" 2>&1 ps -eo pid,ppid,user,comm,args --sort=pid > "$P/v037-post-correctness-processes.txt" printf 'V037_CORRECTNESS_END_UTC=%s\n' "$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S.%NZ)" >> "$P/v037-correctness.log" awk -F '\t' 'NR>1 && $5 != 0 {failed++} END {printf "CORRECTNESS_RUNS=%d FAILED=%d\n", NR-1, failed+0; exit (failed>0)}' "$P/v037-correctness-index.tsv"
